- **cis-eqtl-gene-annotation**: Use when sentinel and proxy variant lists (rsID + GRCh37 coordinates) at molecular QTLs require cis-eQTL target gene annotation from GTEx v7 across user-specified tissues as part of a bottom-up candidate-gene prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cis-eqtl-gene-annotation))
- **cis-eqtl-target-gene-extraction**: Use when you have sentinel/proxy variant rsIDs with GRCh37 coordinates and need to flag which local genes are cis-eQTL targets in GTEx tissues, as bottom-up evidence for causal gene prioritization at molecular QTLs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cis-eqtl-target-gene-extraction))
- **cis-eqtl-variant-filtering**: Use when summary-level eQTL statistics must be converted into per-gene cis-window SNP sets and LD-clumped reference inputs for PRS/TWAS Stage I weight training from summary data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cis-eqtl-variant-filtering))
